## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.31.1rc0: [Metrics] Expose cached prompt tokens by cache tier (#56318)](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.31.1rc0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>Signed-off-by: Cam Quilici <a href="mailto:cjquilici@gmail.com">cjquilici@gmail.com</a><br />
Signed-off-by: Cam Quilici <a href="mailto:cameron@semianalysis.com">cameron@semianalysis.com</a><br />
Co-authored-by: Cam Quilici <a href="mailto:cameron@semianalysis.com">cameron@semianalysis.com</a><br />
Co-authored-by: Nick Hill <a href="mailto:nickhill123@gmail.com">nickhill123@gmail.com</a><br />
Co-authored-by: Yifan Qiao <a href="mailto:yifanqiao@inferact.ai">yifanqiao@inferact.ai</a></p>

---

### [v0.31.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.31.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>v0.31.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 717 commits from 307 contributors (96 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash performance</strong>: FlashMLA mega attention with the V4.1 NVFP4 compressed KV cache is now the SM100 default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56935">#56935</a>); DeepGEMM sparse MQA logits for the indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56254">#56254</a>) and Mega-Gate fusing the gate GEMM with expert selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56266">#56266</a>); decoder boundaries fuse the TP all-reduce, mHC input preparation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57643">#57643</a>) and the MoE finalize (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58586">#58586</a>); a fused small-batch WO-A with inverse RoPE and MXFP8 quant on SM100/SM103 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58634">#58634</a>); the MXFP8 <code>wo_b</code> GEMM fused with the sequence-parallel reduce-scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57428">#57428</a>); Engram <code>wkv</code> sharded across TP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58678">#58678</a>) and Engram host tables shared across co-located DP replicas by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57651">#57651</a>); encoder CUDA graphs for the vision tower (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56625">#56625</a>); and SWA bounded replay that keeps the sliding-window KV out of prefix caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56227">#56227</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fast restart</strong>: the new <code>vllm preload</code> CLI launches the weight-cache daemon that keeps post-quantized weights resident in GPU memory across engine restarts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56680">#56680</a>), now with data parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57386">#57386</a>), MTP draft models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57312">#57312</a>), a <code>/health</code> endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58552">#58552</a>) and a readiness wait (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58370">#58370</a>). Experimental initialized-engine snapshots (<code>vllm snapshot create/restore</code>) use CRIU to restore a fully initialized TP1 engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51360">#51360</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 and speculative decoding</strong>: draft-model speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43091">#43091</a>) and custom logits processors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56497">#56497</a>) on Model Runner V2; the new LiLiCorr drafter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57934">#57934</a>); async scheduling for DFlash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58065">#58065</a>) with the context K/V precompute captured in the draft CUDA graph (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57632">#57632</a>); DSpark adaptive verification for Gemma4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57263">#57263</a>) and variable-length decode for Kimi-K3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52988">#52988</a>); a DCP target with a non-DCP DSpark draft (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56723">#56723</a>); and MoE memory now counted during MRV2 profiling, avoiding OOMs on WideEP deployments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57270">#57270</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58411">#58411</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Large scale serving</strong>: MoonEP balanced EP all2all backend via <code>--all2all-backend moonep</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52101">#52101</a>), prefill context parallelism with data parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57075">#57075</a>), a low-SM multimem reduce-scatter for SM100/SM103 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55072">#55072</a>), DeepEPv2 with sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57210">#57210</a>) and EPLB with shared-expert overlap (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57236">#57236</a>), a sharding-aware NCCL M2N weight-transfer backend for RL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51520">#51520</a>), and KV offloading back-pressure detection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50045">#50045</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Scheduling controls</strong>: <code>--max-num-active-seqs</code> caps RUNNING admission independently of <code>max_num_seqs</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56758">#56758</a>), <code>--long-prefill-token-threshold</code> now adapts to the number of waiting prefills instead of chunking a lone request (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57951">#57951</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58459">#58459</a>), the waiting queue was reworked so requests already holding KV blocks are scheduled first (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58947">#58947</a>), and the KV connector + MTP deadlock under KV pressure was fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57104">#57104</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HiSparse hardening</strong>: MTP verification rows resolved with a union residency kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59235">#59235</a>), MTP acceptance collapse under FULL graphs fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59309">#59309</a>), no GPU pages without host backing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59036">#59036</a>), a chunked-prefill preemption livelock fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59494">#59494</a>), KV cache sized from the groups HiSparse allocates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59450">#59450</a>), and host prefix publication and GPU prefix adoption fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59007">#59007</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59282">#59282</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Security</strong>: per-request <code>mm_processor_kwargs</code> and <code>media_io_kwargs</code> are rejected unless <code>--trust-request-mm-kwargs</code> is set (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58830">#58830</a>); prefix-cache extra keys are tagged by source so a LoRA name and a <code>cache_salt</code> can no longer collide (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51899">#51899</a>), and the LoRA path is part of the block hash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59335">#59335</a>); stale multimodal receiver-cache entries can no longer replace fresh payloads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57833">#57833</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Breaking changes</strong>: per-request multimodal kwargs gated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58830">#58830</a>); <code>tokenizer_mode="slow"</code> removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58545">#58545</a>); <code>--enable-mamba-fine-grained-prefix-cache</code> renamed to <code>--enable-mamba-shared-prefix-checkpoint</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57382">#57382</a>); online quantization through <code>quantization="fp8"</code> replaced by the <code>fp8_per_tensor</code> shorthand (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53585">#53585</a>) and Quark silent online quantization removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51800">#51800</a>); the AllSpark INT8 W8A16 backend removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58001">#58001</a>); <code>--enforce-eager</code> now also disables JIT kernel warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58197">#58197</a>); XPU graphs enabled by default with <code>VLLM_XPU_ENABLE_XPU_GRAPH</code> removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51600">#51600</a>).</li>
</ul>

<h2>Model Support</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New model capabilities</strong>: DiffusionGemma structured generation mode with bounded single-token choices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57250">#57250</a>), MiMo V2 MXFP4 MoE, BF16 MoE router and DFlash drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57784">#57784</a>), Cohere2MoE auxiliary hidden states for EAGLE3/DFlash drafters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49819">#49819</a>), GLM-5.2-MXFP4 on the ROCm DeepSeek-V3.2 path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51915">#51915</a>), AMD-Quark mixed-precision DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-MXFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57071">#57071</a>) and GLM-5.3-Flash Quark MXFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56176">#56176</a>) checkpoints, and a built-in <code>granite_thinking_parser</code> for Granite 4.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55957">#55957</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash</strong>: FlashMLA mega attention with the NVFP4 compressed KV cache as the SM100 default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56935">#56935</a>), DeepGEMM sparse MQA logits in the indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56254">#56254</a>), Mega-Gate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56266">#56266</a>), fused TP all-reduce + mHC input preparation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57643">#57643</a>) with the MoE finalize folded in (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58586">#58586</a>), fused small-batch WO-A (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58634">#58634</a>), MXFP8 <code>wo_b</code> GEMM + reduce-scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" NVFP4 cache gathers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57604">#57604</a>), the fused query RMSNorm + MXFP8 path restored (<a class="issue-link <li><strong>GLM-5.3-Flash</strong>: opt-in FlashAttention and FlashMLA sparse backends on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55385">#55385</a>), NoPE sparse MLA on the FlashInfer SM120 backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55277">#55277</a>), cooperative top-k for small decode batches (<a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57534">#57534</a>), kpool top-k through the shared dispatcher (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58684">#58684</a>), FlashKDA keeping the recurrent state in FP32 for long prefills (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58846">#58846</a>), and correctness fixes for kpool corruption with speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58704">#58704</a>), kpool tail strides (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57477">#57477</a>), 500k-token prompts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57317">#57317</a>), dense MLP layers under sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58594">#58594</a>) and varlen paged MQA launches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55270">#55270</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (Qwen4Exp)</strong>: FP8 main KV cache on the QSA path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55557">#55557</a>), FP8 TP with FlashInfer TRTLLM MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55867">#55867</a>), SM90 QSA tuning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57273">#57273</a>), fused HC down projection + SiLU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58957">#58957</a>), lower PLE metadata overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58114">#58114</a>), QSA indexer workspace fragmentation fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57105">#57105</a>), profiling KV cache released (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58961">#58961</a>), pinned PLE prefetch ids kept out of the CUDA graph pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58489">#58489</a>), and indexed expert-mapping lookups saving about 25 s of weight loading on DGX Spark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58720">#58720</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kimi K3 and MiniMax-M3</strong>: Kimi-K3 vision patch embedder as a GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58527">#58527</a>), fused KimiViT QK RoPE (up to 29x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58651">#58651</a>), routed expert quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57430">#57430</a>), reasoning parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57098">#57098</a>) and reasoning token counting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58372">#58372</a>) fixes, DSpark context KV pointers refreshed after re-binding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58814">#58814</a>), stateless first chunks no longer classified as decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51483">#51483</a>), and Mamba block estimates that no longer stall admission on external prefix hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57050">#57050</a>); MiniMax-M3 encoder CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58673">#58673</a>), <code>Conv3dLayer</code> patch embedding (about 62x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58512">#58512</a>), <code>triton_mrope</code> in the vision tower (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58526">#58526</a>), fused MiniMax2 routing with non-unit scaling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58880">#58880</a>), and processor fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58460">#58460</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59613">#59613</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DiffusionGemma</strong>: one-pass sampler statistics kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58226">#58226</a>), <code>diffusion_constrained</code> reads over <code>logprob_token_ids</code> (about 25% faster, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58216">#58216</a>), fewer logit rows for prefill-only batches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57416">#57416</a>), <code>logprob_token_ids</code> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57417">#57417</a>), and fixes for concurrent logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57414">#57414</a>), eager fallback dtype (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57462">#57462</a>), multimodal inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57589">#57589</a>), quantized LM heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48521">#48521</a>) and CPU execution (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58964">#58964</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>: Triton <code>mm_input_norm</code> kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56798">#56798</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56711">#56711</a>), raw pixels kept through the DP-sharded ViT path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57487">#57487</a>), MiMo-V2.5 fused FP8 <code>qkv_proj</code> sharding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57508">#57508</a>), Gemma4 <code>AutoWeightsLoader</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55911">#55911</a>) and buffer scalars (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54213">#54213</a>), Gemma4 FP8 KV with FA4 at head dim 512 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53175">#53175</a>), Laguna RoPE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57189">#57189</a>), Mistral-Large-3 accuracy regression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57563">#57563</a>), malformed EXIF (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/56527">#56527</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57234">#57234</a>), JinaVL labels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57347">#57347</a>), Sarvam MLA routing with FP32 router logits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56034">#56034</a>), fused CohereASR attention scores (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55190">#55190</a>), and fused DFlash2 grouped convolution (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55960">#55960</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LoRA</strong>: Nemotron VL language models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56231">#56231</a>), ModernBert (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57148">#57148</a>), VoyageQwen3 embeddings (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57708">#57708</a>), RoBERTa sequence classification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58884">#58884</a>), and <code>modules_to_save</code> sequence-classification heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53555">#53555</a>) with per-adapter <code>num_labels</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57766">#57766</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Engine Core</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: draft-model speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43091">#43091</a>), custom logits processors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56497">#56497</a>) validated at admission (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57728">#57728</a>), randomized dummy inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58411">#58411</a>), dummy tokens routed to MoE experts during profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57270">#57270</a>), FULL decode graphs for one-token prompt tails (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58400">#58400</a>), token-to-request mappings shared (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57102">#57102</a>) and Mamba/GDN metadata reused across KV cache groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58762">#58762</a>), encoder-only ViT CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56922">#56922</a>), weight offloader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57834">#57834</a>) and pooling model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57737">#57737</a>) released on shutdown, and fixes for multi-layer MTP KV in P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55055">#55055</a>), fast-prefill with LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56456">#56456</a>), stale block-table writes from dummy draft steps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56734">#56734</a>), padded prompt tails in hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58434">#58434</a>), never-proposed draft slots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58784">#58784</a>), auto-fit <code>max_model_len</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58149">#58149</a>) and <code>intermediate_tensors</code> during capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57745">#57745</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: LiLiCorr drafter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57934">#57934</a>), DFlash async scheduling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58065">#58065</a>) and context K/V in the draft CUDA graph (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57632">#57632</a>), Gemma4 DSpark adaptive verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57263">#57263</a>), Kimi-K3 variable-length adaptive verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52988">#52988</a>), CPU-GPU sync removed for heterogeneous vocabularies (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57396">#57396</a>), Triton recompiles avoided in the acceptance estimator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57107">#57107</a>), and fixes for EAGLE/dense drafts with EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56930">#56930</a>), DFlash/DSpark profiling batches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56448">#56448</a>), GLM MTP head memory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55442">#55442</a>), prompt embeddings with drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57356">#57356</a>), and TritonMLA causal multi-token decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51065">#51065</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Scheduler</strong>: <code>--max-num-active-seqs</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56758">#56758</a>), adaptive <code>--long-prefill-token-threshold</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57951">#57951</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58459">#58459</a>), <code>skipped_waiting</code> replaced by a KV-holding waiting queue (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58947">#58947</a>), atomic admission of <code>n &gt; 1</code> requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53936">#53936</a>), KV connector + MTP deadlock (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57104">#57104</a>), a throughput cliff when <code>max_num_seqs</code> is not a multiple of 8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57355">#57355</a>), streaming continuations keeping logprobs and refreshing max tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57447">#57447</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57676">#57676</a>), a resumable request + async scheduling race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58259">#58259</a>), and a non-blocking structured-output grammar poll (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55931">#55931</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Prefix caching and hybrid models</strong>: extra keys tagged by source (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51899">#51899</a>) and LoRA paths hashed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59335">#59335</a>), <code>--enable-mamba-shared-prefix-checkpoint</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57382">#57382</a>), prompt-tail hits with MTP restored (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58368">#58368</a>), prompt-end checkpoints kept under sparse retention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59146">#59146</a>), align-mode checkpoint reservation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59175">#59175</a>), stateless GDN first chunks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51565">#51565</a>), MTP draft KV cache groups annotated on the hybrid grouping path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55390">#55390</a>), a generalized prefill checkpoint builder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57783">#57783</a>), batched Mamba2 prefill state saves without GPU-CPU syncs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49371">#49371</a>), <code>skip_reading_prefix_cache</code> honored for connector hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57269">#57269</a>), incremental multimodal block hashing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51694">#51694</a>), a KV block size every attention backend supports (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49845">#49845</a>) with clearer errors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58557">#58557</a>), and <code>CacheConfig.effective_attention_block_size</code> for DCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56538">#56538</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Startup and memory</strong>: parallel Triton warmup compilation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58582">#58582</a>), JIT warmup disabled under <code>--enforce-eager</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58197">#58197</a>) unless fault tolerance is on (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58593">#58593</a>), DeepGEMM warmup reusing the MoE workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57268">#57268</a>), allocator fragmentation no longer shrinking the KV cache during profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58430">#58430</a>), sparse prefill buffers reserved before KV sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57575">#57575</a>), stale FlashMLA workspace views released (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56902">#56902</a>), DeepGEMM FP8 workspace halved (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53914">#53914</a>), shared Marlin/Humming workspaces (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57421">#57421</a>), FlashInfer BF16 MoE weights converted in place (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54699">#54699</a>), UniProc startup threads bounded by the CPU quota (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58946">#58946</a>), and runtime threads set before profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55891">#55891</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels</strong>: sampled filtering for persistent top-k (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56346">#56346</a>), Murmur3 RNG for Gumbel sampling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51367">#51367</a>), register-resident per-token-group 8-bit quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55330">#55330</a>), vectorized per-tensor FP8 abs-max (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58194">#58194</a>), a Triton kernel dispatcher for platform-specific overrides (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43048">#43048</a>), <code>GateLinear</code> for all MoE models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58234">#58234</a>), non-local expert slots skipped in <code>TritonExperts</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58051">#58051</a>), deferred TRT-LLM-Gen top-k finalize on the modular path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58635">#58635</a>), SM120 batch-invariant matmul configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57456">#57456</a>), FlashInfer prefill dequant scratch bounded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57918">#57918</a>), and fixes for Triton softcap NaNs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56579">#56579</a>), QuIP Hadamard transforms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43462">#43462</a>), CUTLASS FP8 linear on A100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55884">#55884</a>), FlashInfer sampling on unsupported GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48956">#48956</a>), SM100 FP8 blockwise scale padding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57377">#57377</a>), mixed FULL graph prefill capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58275">#58275</a>) Inductor custom-op pattern matching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58189">#58189</a>), CuteDSL BF16 GDN prefill diverging from FLA on Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53864">#53864</a>), SM100 <code>fp8_ds_mla</code> cache scales (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49435">#49435</a>), ragged decode batches in the sparse indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52500">#52500</a>), stale <code>allowed_token_ids</code> masks after batch reordering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48419">#48419</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43931">#43931</a>), and <code>prompt_embeds</code> tensors held after requests finish (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57988">#57988</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Batch invariance</strong>: breakable CUDA graphs without torch.compile by default under <code>VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57586">#57586</a>), sequence parallelism and async TP disabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56377">#56377</a>), and NCCL 2.31 collectives kept enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58179">#58179</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Sleep and RL</strong>: <code>release_kv_cache_memory()</code> frees only KV cache memory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44890">#44890</a>), <code>--sleep-preserve-parameter-names</code> retains frozen weights across level-2 sleep (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57891">#57891</a>), NCCL M2N weight transfer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51520">#51520</a>), dense DP weight updates by DP index (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56950">#56950</a>), allocator config kept when toggling expandable segments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57982">#57982</a>), and cache reset failures propagated during sleep (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54581">#54581</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fast restart</strong>: <code>vllm preload</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56680">#56680</a>), DP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57386">#57386</a>), MTP drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57312">#57312</a>), readiness wait (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58370">#58370</a>), <code>/health</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58552">#58552</a>), ModelOpt MXFP8 pre-processed weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57316">#57316</a>), and initialized-engine snapshots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51360">#51360</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Logging and observability</strong>: <code>LoggingConfig</code> via <code>--logging-config</code> and <code>--log-level</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57205">#57205</a>), JSON logging fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57957">#57957</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58747">#58747</a>), startup log suppression fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51366">#51366</a>), unified platform-aware torch profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57460">#57460</a>), no 0.0% prefix cache hit rate before any query (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54990">#54990</a>), KV transfer metrics formatting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57068">#57068</a>), MFU activation sizing from the model dtype (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57070">#57070</a>), platform-overridable env var checks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48599">#48599</a>), <code>VLLM_TARGET_DEVICE=empty pip install vllm</code> for out-of-tree backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41074">#41074</a>), and the correct vLLM version reported when installing from source (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57295">#57295</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57744">#57744</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Large Scale Serving</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>MoE communication</strong>: MoonEP backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52101">#52101</a>), DeepEPv2 with sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57210">#57210</a>) and EPLB + shared-expert overlap (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57236">#57236</a>), low-SM multimem reduce-scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55072">#55072</a>), EPLB load statistics during Elastic EP scaling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58473">#58473</a>), SP padded rows skipped in grouped routing so rank 0 is no longer a prefill straggler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56079">#56079</a>), hash routing rejected on unsupported monolithic backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57867">#57867</a>), sampled-token broadcasts skipped under PP for requests leaving the engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58542">#58542</a>), DBO with DeepEP low-latency profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57502">#57502</a>), external LB with replicas sharing nodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53743">#53743</a>), and no blocking RPC during the engine handshake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57226">#57226</a>) or unbounded draft-token waits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58779">#58779</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Context parallelism</strong>: PCP with DP, EP and MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57075">#57075</a>), a DCP target with non-DCP DSpark/DFlash drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56723">#56723</a>), DCP sequence lengths without a CPU-GPU sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58169">#58169</a>), and NIXL DCP pulls across MLA cache regions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57389">#57389</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connectors</strong>: NIXL pipeline-parallel push prefill for attention-HMA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50494">#50494</a>) and packed MLA layouts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50499">#50499</a>), transport-failure metrics split from KV expiry (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55854">#55854</a>), dead peer state released without waiting for TTL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50047">#50047</a>), expired leases reaped behind a heartbeated head (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58292">#58292</a>), push completion restored (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58188">#58188</a>), D-side activity recorded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52245">#52245</a>); Mooncake <code>CUSTOM_MEM_POOL</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49300">#49300</a>), request-level load failures under HMA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56855">#56855</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57174">#57174</a>) and bootstrap retries (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58919">#58919</a>); MoRIIO hybrid Mamba/KDA state in READ mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51052">#51052</a>) and a multi-decode routing race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51681">#51681</a>); prefill cache hits in <code>prompt_tokens_details</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54222">#54222</a>); KV-event publishers bound at port 0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55844">#55844</a>); KV cache metadata GET restored for external consumers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56925">#56925</a>); partial-block KV events keep every multimodal feature (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58288">#58288</a>); saves finalized on steps without a forward (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57775">#57775</a>); abort-safe <code>ExampleHiddenStatesConnector</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56841">#56841</a>); DecodeBench FP8 fills (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58472">#58472</a>); a <code>KvHints</code> request envelope (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53423">#53423</a>); and the AuxOutput connector for routed-expert outputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45635">#45635</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58150">#58150</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58205">#58205</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV offloading</strong>: per-request <code>max_load_tokens</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55885">#55885</a>), back-pressure detection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50045">#50045</a>), <code>SimpleCPUOffloadConnector</code> Prometheus metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57251">#57251</a>), non-prefix-cacheable (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56810">#56810</a>) and scratch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57145">#57145</a>) groups skipped, replicated layouts for multi-group MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57652">#57652</a>), regions of 512 GiB or more registered in chunks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51081">#51081</a>), cgroup memory checked before SHM allocation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54014">#54014</a>), and fixes for cache recency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51787">#51787</a>), MTP-retained sliding windows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56709">#56709</a>), canonical MLA rows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56799">#56799</a>), event metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57453">#57453</a>) and ROCm pinned memory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57160">#57160</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HiSparse</strong>: union residency kernel for MTP rows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59235">#59235</a>), MTP acceptance under FULL graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59309">#59309</a>), host-backed allocation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59036">#59036</a>), preemption livelock (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59494">#59494</a>), KV cache sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59450">#59450</a>), host prefix publication (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59007">#59007</a>), GPU prefix adoption (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59282">#59282</a>), and residency metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58725">#58725</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Encoder disaggregation (EPD)</strong>: dynamic EPD proxy with launcher-managed registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54176">#54176</a>), image requests batched per encoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57095">#57095</a>), cross-encoder caching through Mooncake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56242">#56242</a>), metadata-only audio inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57887">#57887</a>), language-model shards skipped for <code>--mm-encoder-only</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58086">#58086</a>), EC connector metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54960">#54960</a>), and encoder-only fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58490">#58490</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58287">#58287</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57696">#57696</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Hardware &amp; Performance</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>: FlashInfer 0.7.0.post1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58069">#58069</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59323">#59323</a>), DeepGEMM pin bump with SM120 fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57218">#57218</a>), Rubin CUDA 13.4 nightly images (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55953">#55953</a>), and QuTLASS builds with PyTorch 2.13 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58173">#58173</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>: AITER v0.1.23 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56885">#56885</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55316">#55316</a>), W4A16 Whisper (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58268">#58268</a>), wheels built on Ubuntu 22.04 (glibc 2.34) with AMX-FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58515">#58515</a>), AVX10.2 gated on compiler support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58133">#58133</a>), pre-built Triton CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58140">#58140</a>), <code>--device-memory-utilization</code> alias (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56547">#56547</a>), vLLM Recipes in the CPU image (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58796">#58796</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57306">#57306</a>), s390x protobuf pin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54978">#54978</a>) and torchcodec video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58693">#58693</a>), and fixes for Ministral FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56985">#56985</a>), FP32 router weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56168">#56168</a>), zentorch import failures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54923">#54923</a>), macOS multimodal SHM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57142">#57142</a>), CPU affinity per local rank (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53636">#53636</a>) and NIXL GDN state layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53300">#53300</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Quantization</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Humming</strong>: Hadamard transforms and NVFP4/MXFP4/MXFP8 online quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56685">#56685</a>), asymmetric wNaM through compressed-tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46528">#46528</a>), humming-kernels 0.1.16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58054">#58054</a>), and Humming in the W4A8 (INT4xFP8) MoE oracle (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58427">#58427</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New capabilities</strong>: native Quark W4A16 INT4/UINT4 exports (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48606">#48606</a>), opt-in load-time MXFP4 dequantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50814">#50814</a>), explicit per-token NVFP4 MoE backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57176">#57176</a>), and a canonical N-first layout for compressed-tensors WNA16 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52798">#52798</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: MXFP8 on layers below <code>mm_mxfp8</code> shape limits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54223">#54223</a>), online NVFP4 scales on reload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57954">#57954</a>), LM head linear metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58444">#58444</a>), and the fused SiLU-mul block-quant path skipped under a SwiGLU clamp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57984">#57984</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>API &amp; Frontend</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New options and endpoints</strong>: <code>--tool-strict-level</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56268">#56268</a>), <code>response_format</code> with <code>tool_choice=auto</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56086">#56086</a>), DeepSeek-V4 FIM completions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44229">#44229</a>), <code>release_kv_cache_memory()</code> and <code>POST /release_kv_cache_memory</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44890">#44890</a>), fixed-token prefill scoring via <code>prompt_logprob_token_ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54335">#54335</a>), per-request speculative decoding metrics in <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43310">#43310</a>), per-request metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55084">#55084</a>) and <code>cache_write_tokens</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57222">#57222</a>) in the Responses API, Anthropic <code>thinking</code> in <code>/v1/messages</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58613">#58613</a>), streaming reasoning and tool calls from the derender endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50550">#50550</a>) with offloaded detokenization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57528">#57528</a>), <code>vllm chat</code> streaming thinking output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57045">#57045</a>), request body debug logging with <code>--enable-log-requests</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58163">#58163</a>), and only the summary line of config docstrings in <code>--help</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57357">#57357</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Structured output and parsers</strong>: native Lark grammars in the xgrammar backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58321">#58321</a>), XGrammar 0.2.7 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57272">#57272</a>), Granite migrated to the streaming Parser Engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49648">#49648</a>), and fixes for reasoning boundaries (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56635">#56635</a>), xgrammar choices with control characters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48115">#48115</a>), list-typed JSON Schema (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48416">#48416</a>), empty <code>structural_tag</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47450">#47450</a>), outlines EOS handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58612">#58612</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57743">#57743</a>), parser-suppressed streaming logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58583">#58583</a>), Inkling tool names after reasoning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58792">#58792</a>), and length <code>finish_reason</code> for truncated streaming tool calls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46303">#46303</a>).</li>
<li><strong>OpenAI, Anthropic and Harmony compatibility</strong>: reasoning token counts for Harmony, DeepSeek-V3 and Step3 (#58626) and per Responses tool round (#58927), Harmony <code>max_output_tokens</code> in the tool loop (#58551), batched chat completions using the adjusted requests (#58929, #58958), a fresh parser per choice (#58939), deferred reasoning recounts (#56067), Responses MCP cleanup (#56988) and image <code>detail</code> default (#57241), Anthropic inline system detection (#58754) and disabled thinking with P/D (#58786), stop strings rejected on <code>--tokens-only</code> servers (#57058), invalid <code>prompt_embeds</code> returning 400 (#55451, #57006), prompts bounded after multimodal expansion (#57076), <code>--override-generation-config</code> penalties (#50769), Hub revisions (#56092, #57461), full logprobs in token-in/token-out responses (#58488), generative scoring cancellation (#57729, #58788), gRPC keepalive pings (#55102), and <code>run-batch</code> diarized transcriptions (#57948).</li>
<li><strong>Pooling</strong>: chunked embedding padding (#56505) and normalization (#57498), reranker tokenization with document limits (#57666), and BERT-family heads kept for raw logits (#57664).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong>: <code>--hf-overrides</code> (#56931), <code>--sse-keep-alive-interval</code> (#58306), custom chat roles (#58311), MiMo V2.5 parsers (#57933), normalized reasoning controls (#56998), parser-owned output grammars (#55269, #57340), sampling masks over gRPC (#56777), local DP size in gRPC metadata with <code>vllm-proto</code> 0.3.0 (#57116, #57233), a per-request preemption histogram (#57033), lock-free histograms (#58574), Nemotron-H vision context (#57634), model-owned vision processors (#58109), an <code>mm-processor</code> benchmark (#51922, #58084, #58378), unsupported serve args recognized (#58330), NaN logprobs no longer killing the engine client (#51026), appended <code>EngineCoreOutput</code> fields accepted (#56533), and <code>vllm-rs</code> on <code>PATH</code> in the CUDA image (#57606).</li>
<li><strong>Benchmarks</strong>: an <code>openai-responses</code> backend for <code>vllm bench serve</code> (#54628) and <code>model_id</code> in latency/throughput JSON (#58112).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Security</h2>
<ul>
<li>Per-request <code>mm_processor_kwargs</code> and <code>media_io_kwargs</code> are rejected by default; trusted deployments opt in with <code>--trust-request-mm-kwargs</code> (#58830).</li>
<li>Prefix-cache block hashes tag extra keys by source (#51899) and include the LoRA path (#59335); multimodal hash input is framed (#54283) and incremental block hashing covers every overlapping feature (#51694).</li>
<li>Fresh multimodal payloads take precedence over a stale receiver cache (#57833), and encoder-cache hits with mismatched embedding counts are rejected (#57696).</li>
<li><code>min_tokens</code> above the filled <code>max_tokens</code> default is rejected instead of wedging the engine (#57731); message sanitization filters upper-case memory addresses (#58832); LoRA adapters named after a served model are rejected (#59286).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Dependencies</h2>
<ul>
<li>FlashInfer 0.7.0.post1 (#58069, #59323), Transformers 5.17.0 (#56108) with an upper bound in requirements (#59614), XGrammar 0.2.7 (#57272), <code>oss-harmony</code> replacing <code>openai-harmony</code> (#55128), DeepGEMM fork pin bump (#57218), FlashKDA bump (#58846), and humming-kernels 0.1.16 (#58054).</li>
<li>CUDA 12 images use LMCache 0.4.4 and CuPy CUDA 12 (#57945); a separately tagged zstd Docker Hub image (<code>-x86_64-zstd</code>) is published (#55608); Rubin CUDA 13.4 nightly images (#55953).</li>
<li>ROCm: AITER v0.1.23 (#58867), torch 2.13, Triton 3.8 (#50605, #58006), patched ROCR (#57328), LMCache OpenTelemetry pins (#59056).</li>
<li>XPU: PyTorch 2.14 (#56013). CPU: wheels built on Ubuntu 22.04 (#58515), pre-built Triton CPU (#58140).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Breaking Changes &amp; Deprecations</h2>
<ul>
<li>Per-request <code>mm_processor_kwargs</code> and <code>media_io_kwargs</code> now return an error unless the server is started with <code>--trust-request-mm-kwargs</code>; server-level <code>--mm-processor-kwargs</code> / <code>--media-io-kwargs</code> and offline <code>LLM</code> are unchanged (#58830).</li>
<li><code>tokenizer_mode="slow"</code> was removed; it already behaved like <code>"hf"</code> under Transformers v5 (#58545).</li>
<li><code>--enable-mamba-fine-grained-prefix-cache</code> was renamed to <code>--enable-mamba-shared-prefix-checkpoint</code> (#57382).</li>
<li>Online quantization through <code>quantization="fp8"</code> now redirects to the <code>fp8_per_tensor</code> online shorthand (#53585); Quark-specific silent online MXFP4 quantization was removed in favor of the online quantization API (#51800).</li>
<li>The AllSpark INT8 W8A16 GEMM backend was removed (#58001). The <code>return_assistant_tokens_mask</code> option of <code>/render</code> and the <code>assistant_tokens_mask</code> response field were removed (#57520).</li>
<li><code>VLLM_PLE_CPU_OFFLOAD</code> was removed; use <code>--engram-config</code> (#57937). <code>VLLM_XPU_ENABLE_XPU_GRAPH</code> was removed and XPU graphs are on by default (#51600).</li>
<li>The comma-separated form of <code>--collect-detailed-traces</code> was removed; use the list syntax (#55702).</li>
<li>New defaults: <code>--enforce-eager</code> also disables JIT kernel warmup unless fault tolerance is enabled (#58197, #58593); <code>VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1</code> uses breakable CUDA graphs without torch.compile (#57586) and disables sequence parallelism and async TP (#56377); DeepSeek-V4.1 SWA bounded replay is on (#56227) except on ROCm (#57906); Engram host tables are shared across co-located DP replicas when possible (#57651); FlashMLA mega attention is the DeepSeek-V4.1 default on SM100 (#56935); the AITER w4a4 ASM GEMM is off by default on ROCm (#57055).</li>
<li>Model Runner V1 + PP &gt; 1 + async scheduling + structured output is now rejected at startup (#56250); <code>json_object</code> is rejected at validation with the outlines backend (#57743).</li>
<li><code>transformers</code> now has an upper bound in requirements (#59614).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.30.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.30.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>v0.30.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 762 commits from 315 contributors (104 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56214">#56214</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56228">#56228</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56208">#56208</a>) with the whole KV stored in MXFP8 through the FlashMLA V4.1 record on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56893">#56893</a>), DeepGEMM Mega-mHC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56962">#56962</a>), and async Engram prefetch with Engram DP sharding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56512">#56512</a>); DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54566">#54566</a>), also on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55107">#55107</a>) and with LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55897">#55897</a>); GLM-5.3-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53906">#53906</a>) with EPLB (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55119">#55119</a>); K2-Horizon (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55063">#55063</a>); Cohere Compass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54774">#54774</a>); Bailing V3 VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55921">#55921</a>); Nanbeige4.2 via the Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56071">#56071</a>); and a DeepSeek-V4 CPU backend with AVX512/AMX sparse MLA, indexer, mHC and compressor kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55355">#55355</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fast Start</strong>: a persistent per-GPU weight-cache daemon holds post-quantized, TP-sharded weights in GPU memory so restarting engines map them over CUDA IPC with <code>--load-format ipc_cache</code> instead of reloading from disk (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54921">#54921</a>), now covering FP4 checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55465">#55465</a>) and multi-node TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55468">#55468</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Watermarking</strong>: Gumbel-max watermarked generation and detection with a keyed PRF, per-request opt-out and an example detection endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54053">#54053</a>); dual-key Gumbel-max makes it compatible with speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56122">#56122</a>); the Rust frontend forwards the per-request controls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56338">#56338</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HiSparse</strong>: a host-resident tier for sparse-MLA decode that spills KV pages to pinned host memory under GPU pressure and serves top-k misses from a per-request GPU hot buffer, enabled through <code>HiSparseConnector</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53781">#53781</a>), with Prometheus counters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56061">#56061</a>), a host cache shared across TP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56629">#56629</a>), and the attention config inferred from the connector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57041">#57041</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: dual-batch overlap in eager mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50945">#50945</a>) and with FULL CUDA graphs for microbatched steps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51700">#51700</a>); MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46994">#46994</a>) and EAGLE3/DFlash/DSpark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50514">#50514</a>) speculative decoding under pipeline parallelism; adaptive verification for every draft-model speculator through an online acceptance estimator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52228">#52228</a>); gc frozen during graph capture, cutting capture from 12s to 2s and engine init from 28.9s to 8.2s on H200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54646">#54646</a>); <code>--return-sampling-mask</code> compacted on GPU, fixing an about 2x RL step-time regression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54901">#54901</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Qwen3.8-Flash-Next performance</strong>: separate prefill and decode QSA indexer kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54513">#54513</a>), fused PLE kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54517">#54517</a>), FP8 indexer cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54890">#54890</a>), padded-index skipping in sparse GQA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54873">#54873</a>), fused PLE residual and QSA output gate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55309">#55309</a>), UVA PLE offload and Engram tensor parallelism via <code>--engram-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54371">#54371</a>), and torch.compile removed from the NVIDIA implementation so FP8 fits on a single GB300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55272">#55272</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kimi K3 performance</strong>: native CUDA AttnRes default on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54261">#54261</a>), KDA mixed-batch gather/scatter removed (5.2-7.7% E2E throughput, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56159">#56159</a>), grouped FP8 MLA cache insertion (4-6x kernel speedup at small batch, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55356">#55356</a>), DSV3 low-latency GEMM on strided tensors (12-81% kernel speedup, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54565">#54565</a>), overlapped TP8 KDA projections (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54697">#54697</a>), FlashInfer KDA kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55364">#55364</a>), internal prefix checkpoints with partial prefix caching and speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53614">#53614</a>), and symmetric DCP disaggregation for hybrid Mamba models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55531">#55531</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Large scale serving</strong>: PCP+DCP on sparse-MLA models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56157">#56157</a>), PCP with single-module MTP and replicated DSpark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56107">#56107</a>) and decode-only FULL CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53867">#53867</a>), Elastic EP reusing CUDA graphs across reconfiguration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54985">#54985</a>), an opt-in FlashInfer PCIe IPC all-reduce for NVLink-less boxes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53576">#53576</a>), DeepEP v2 async finalize overlapping shared experts with combine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52781">#52781</a>), Mooncake Store heterogeneous TP sharing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53129">#53129</a>), a KVCR secondary-tier adapter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53624">#53624</a>), and encoder-cache sharing over NIXL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47941">#47941</a>) and Mooncake (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41567">#41567</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Quantization</strong>: targeted online quantization through <code>quantization_config.targets</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51285">#51285</a>) and on partially pre-quantized checkpoints from any quant method (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51392">#51392</a>), W4A16 DSA with the <code>nvfp4_fp8_ds_mla</code> KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51724">#51724</a>), FlashInfer CuTeDSL NVFP4 W4A16 default over Marlin on SM100/103 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53014">#53014</a>), NVFP4 in the torch linear backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53319">#53319</a>), per-quantization linear backend overrides (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51204">#51204</a>), AutoRound 2/3/5/6/7-bit on CUDA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52890">#52890</a>), and DeepSelect top-k for the DSA sparse indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56464">#56464</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Breaking changes</strong>: scale-out endpoints are opt-in on plain <code>vllm serve</code> via <code>--enable-scale-out</code>, replacing <code>VLLM_ENABLE_SCALE_OUT_ENDPOINTS</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54579">#54579</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55176">#55176</a>); GPTQ activation ordering (<code>g_idx</code>) removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54809">#54809</a>); items deprecated for 0.29 removed, including the <code>VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL</code> and <code>VLLM_MM_HASHER_ALGORITHM</code> env vars (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55353">#55353</a>); the <code>all</code> Mamba cache mode deprecated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55041">#55041</a>); <code>python -m vllm.entrypoints.grpc_server</code> deprecated in favor of <code>vllm serve --grpc</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56746">#56746</a>); YaRN aligned with Transformers so vendor YaRN aliases no longer re-scale <code>max_model_len</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56446">#56446</a>).</li>
</ul>

<h3>Other Artifacts</h3>
<p>Pre-built release artifacts are available in the <strong>Assets</strong> section at the bottom of this page, including:</p>
<ul>
<li>Source distribution tarball</li>
